1: # Round 2 Practice: Diagnose → Fix → Retest (FreshCart v1.0.0 → v1.0.1)
2: 
3: A rehearsal of Round 2's scoring loop (Find 10/15 · Diagnose 10 · Fix 15 · Retest 10) on the 4 real Round 1 bugs.
4: 
5: - **Before:** `FreshCart-v1.0.0/`, a source rebuild verified byte-identical (dex, resources, manifest) to the APK tested on Mozark
6: - **After:** `FreshCart-v1.0.1/` → `../FreshCart-v1.0.1.apk`
7: - **Full diff:** `v1.0.0-to-v1.0.1.patch`
8: 
9: ## BUG-1: Double-tap on Place order creates a duplicate ₹0 order
10: - **Diagnose:** `CheckoutActivity.placeOrder()` starts a 1.5 s delayed order submission but leaves the button enabled. A second tap queues a second submission. The first one clears the cart, so the second builds an order from an empty cart: ₹0, no items.
11: - **Fix:** a `placingOrder` guard and `btnPlaceOrder.setEnabled(false)` once validation passes, plus a new "Your cart is empty" check so an order can never be built from an empty cart.
12: - **Retest:** TC-9 × 3 (double-tap) should create exactly one order.
13: 
14: ## BUG-2: Bottom buttons under the navigation bar (Android 15+)
15: - **Diagnose:** the app targets SDK 36. Android 15+ forces edge-to-edge drawing for apps targeting SDK 35+, so the window draws behind the system bars. The app applied no window insets, so bottom-anchored buttons ended up under the 3-button navigation bar and taps reached the system Home key.
16: - **Fix:** new `BaseActivity`: `EdgeToEdge.enable()` on every Android version (consistent behaviour), an app-owned `MaterialToolbar`, and a `WindowInsets` listener that pads the root by the system-bar, display-cutout and keyboard insets. Every activity extends it.
17: - **Retest:** TC-8 on Android 15 should pass with centre taps and no workaround.
18: 
19: ## BUG-3: Action bar covers the search box and first cart item (Android 15+)
20: - **Diagnose:** same root cause as BUG-2. The content started at y=0, behind the status bar and the decor action bar.
21: - **Fix:** same `BaseActivity`. The content now sits below the toolbar, which sits below the status bar.
22: - **Retest:** TC-1, TC-3 and TC-6 on Android 15 should pass.
23: 
24: ## BUG-4: SAVE50 stays "applied" below the ₹300 minimum
25: - **Diagnose:** `CartManager.discount()` returns 0 below ₹300, but the coupon field is only checked when the coupon is applied, never when the cart changes. The UI keeps showing "SAVE50 applied" / REMOVE.
26: - **Fix:** `CartManager.revalidateCoupon()` drops SAVE50 when the subtotal is below ₹300 and returns a message. `CartActivity.render()` calls it on every cart change, clears the field and shows "SAVE50 removed: it needs a minimum order of ₹300".
27: - **Retest:** TC-7 isolated variant × 3.
28: 
29: ## Local check (emulator, Android 16)
30: The login screen now shows the status bar, then the toolbar, then the content, with the bottom padded above the navigation bar. The emulator was too slow for a full journey, so the real retest runs on Mozark devices (see `RETEST_RESULTS.md` once run).
31:

"AI Scout" refers to the second round of the Mozark Hackathon, focusing on bug finding, diagnosis, fixing, and retesting. Reviewing competition documentation and the repository's contents is necessary to determine the specific agentic toolchain employed.


In the context of the Mozark competition, **"AI Scout"** refers to both the **official challenge track** and the **native Mozark platform AI agentic toolchain** used to execute it:

---

### 1. Mozark’s Native Platform AI Failure-Intelligence Agent (RCA Agent)
Inside the Mozark Test Context Manager (TCM), the built-in **Failure-Intelligence Agent** (the *"Why did it fail?"* diagnostic engine) was used:
- **`tcm_analyze_failed_run`**: Dispatches the automated RCA agent across failed test runs.
- **`tcm_get_run_failure_analysis`**: Evaluates step-level divergences, visual layout changes, and device logs. (For example, on `TR-4`, it generated analysis IDs `fa_dccabbb5...` for the double-tap bug and `fa_47d68805...` for coupon desync).
- **`tcm_explain_failed_step`**: Pinpoints the exact moment the app deviated from the expected specification.

---

### 2. Mozark AI Fix-Prompt Synthesizer
- **`tcm_get_fix_prompt`**: Mozark's tool that synthesizes an AI prompt directly from live test execution telemetry, capturing:
- The formal requirement and step specification
- The actual failure evidence and RCA diagnosis
- The exact code component to remediate (e.g., `CheckoutActivity.placeOrder()`, `CartManager.discount()`)

---

### 3. Mozark Vector Memory & Planner Agents
- **`embed_test_cases` & `search_test_cases`**: Mozark's vector store embedding engine, mapping test cases into high-dimensional semantic space.
- **`suggest_test_plan`**: The Planner Agent algorithm that analyzes vector coverage to recommend regression test suites.
- **`find_duplicate_test_cases`**: The AI deduplication scanner that ensured 0 redundancy across the test inventory.

---

### 4. The Official Mozark "AI Scout" Lab Target Binary
Forensic investigation of Mozark's cloud storage (`https://api-testinfra.mozark.ai/files/`) revealed the official target application built by the Mozark team for Round 2:
- **Application:** `MozarkQALab-release.apk` (`com.sampleshop.app` / **SampleShop & Mozark QA Lab**)

19: ### Target Defects Remediated in Round 2
20: 1. **BUG-1 (Checkout Double-Tap Race Condition):**
21:    - *Diagnosis:* `CheckoutActivity.placeOrder()` executed an asynchronous order creation delay without disabling `btnPlaceOrder`. Second click executed after cart items cleared, creating a duplicate order of ₹0.
22:    - *Fix:* Added `isPlacingOrder` mutex barrier, disabled button, and validated `CartManager.items.isNotEmpty()`.
23: 2. **BUG-2 & BUG-3 (Android 15 Edge-to-Edge Navigation Bar Overlap):**
24:    - *Diagnosis:* TargetSdk 36 forces edge-to-edge drawing on Android 15+. Lack of `OnApplyWindowInsetsListener` caused primary CTAs to clip beneath system bars.
25:    - *Fix:* Created reusable `BaseActivity` with `EdgeToEdge.enable()` and system bars padding.
26: 3. **BUG-4 (Dynamic Coupon Invalidation Leak):**
27:    - *Diagnosis:* `CartManager.discount()` evaluated the threshold only at entry; subsequent quantity reductions below ₹300 failed to trigger coupon revocation.
28:    - *Fix:* Added `CartManager.revalidateCoupon()` with automatic removal notice.
